<script type="text/javascript">
    //Email Capture For Quote

    var emailCapture = Class.create();
    emailCapture.prototype = {
        initialize: function(options) {
            this.options = Object.extend({
                elements: "input[name='billing[email]']"
            }, options || {} );

            // Observe blur on email field
            $$(this.options.elements).each(function(email) {
                Event.observe(email,'blur',this.captureEmail.bindAsEventListener(this));
            }.bind(this));
        },

        //capture email
        captureEmail: function(event) {
            var el = $(Event.element(event));
            //validate email
            if(el.value && this.validateEmail(el.value)){
                //ajax call
                new Ajax.Request('https://www.screwfix.eu/' + 'connector/ajax/emailcapture', {
                    method: 'post',
                    parameters:{
                        email:el.value
                    }
                });
            }
        },

        //email validation
        validateEmail: function (email) {
            var re = /^(([^<>()[\]\\.,;:\s@\"]+(\.[^<>()[\]\\.,;:\s@\"]+)*)|(\".+\"))@((\[[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\])|(([a-zA-Z\-0-9]+\.)+[a-zA-Z]{2,}))$/;
            return re.test(email);
        }
    };

    //Email Capture For Newsletter

    var newsletterEmailCapture = Class.create();
    newsletterEmailCapture.prototype = {
        initialize: function(options) {
            this.options = Object.extend({
                elements: "input[name='email']"
            }, options || {} );

            // Observe blur on email field
            $$(this.options.elements).each(function(email) {
                Event.observe(email,'blur',this.captureEmail.bindAsEventListener(this));
            }.bind(this));
        },

        //capture email
        captureEmail: function(event) {
            var el = $(Event.element(event));
            //validate email
            if(el.value && this.validateEmail(el.value)){
                //ajax call
                new Ajax.Request('https://www.screwfix.eu/' + 'connector/ajax/emailcapture', {
                    method: 'post',
                    parameters:{
                        email:el.value
                    }
                });
            }
        },

        //email validation
        validateEmail: function (email) {
            var re = /^(([^<>()[\]\\.,;:\s@\"]+(\.[^<>()[\]\\.,;:\s@\"]+)*)|(\".+\"))@((\[[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\])|(([a-zA-Z\-0-9]+\.)+[a-zA-Z]{2,}))$/;
            return re.test(email);
        }
    };
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
//<![CDATA[
    function switchStoreView(oldStoreCode, newStoreCode)
    {
        // grab the current URL to amend
        var newHref = window.location.href;

        // if the params already exist, replace with our new value
        if (newHref.indexOf("___store") >= 0) {
            newHref = newHref.replace(/(___store=)[^\&]+/, '$1' + newStoreCode);
        }
        if (newHref.indexOf("___from_store") >= 0) {
            newHref = newHref.replace(/(___from_store=)[^\&]+/, '$1' + oldStoreCode);
        }

        // if the params don't already exist, add them
        if (newHref.indexOf("___store") < 0) {
            newHref += (newHref.indexOf("?") > -1) ? '&' : '?';
            newHref += "___store=" + newStoreCode;
        }
        if (newHref.indexOf("___from_store") < 0) {
            newHref += (newHref.indexOf("?") > -1) ? '&' : '?';
            newHref += "___from_store=" + oldStoreCode;
        }

        if (newHref.indexOf("nocache") < 0) {
            newHref += (newHref.indexOf("?") > -1) ? '&' : '?';
            newHref += "nocache=" + (new Date()).getTime();
        }
        if (newHref.indexOf("nocache") >= 0) {
            newHref = newHref.replace(/(nocache=)[^\&]+/, '$1' + (new Date()).getTime());
        }

        // redirect the customer to the new location
        window.location.href = newHref;

        return true;
    }

    // Remove __store, __from_store, nocache parameters
    // after store switch (while on homepage)
    var params = window.location.search;
    if (window.location.pathname === '/' && params !== ''
        && typeof window.history.replaceState === 'function')
    {
        params = params
            .replace(/(\?|&)___store=default(_tax|)/, '')
            .replace(/(\?|&)___from_store=default(_tax|)/, '')
            .replace(/(\?|&)nocache=\d+/, '');

        if (params === '') {
            window.history.replaceState('', '', '/');
        }
    }

//]]>
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
        //<![CDATA[
        jQuery(function searchFormAutoComplete($) {
            $('#search_autocomplete').on('click', '.fsm_auto_searchItem', function(event) {
                event.preventDefault();
                var self = $(this);
                var search = $('#search');
                var searchString = self.text();
                search.val(searchString);
                $('#search_mini_form').trigger('submit');
            });
            $('#search').fsmAutocomplete({
                dropDownSelector: '#search_autocomplete',
                buildPopularProducts: function (products, input) {
                    var html = [];
                    for (var ii = 0; ii < products.length; ii++) {
                        var title = products[ii]["title"];
                        var sku = products[ii]["sku"];
                        var url = products[ii]["product_url"];
                        var image = products[ii]["thumbnail_url"];
                        html.push('<div class="fsm_auto_productItem" data-fsm-auto-type="product"><a href="' + url + '">' + title + '<div class="fsm_auto_productImg"><img src="' + image + '"/></div></a></div>');
                    };
                    $(this.productsSelector).html(html.join(""));
                }
            });
        });
        //]]>
    </script>

<script>
var closeButton = document.querySelector('.modal__close')

function showModal() {
  // Close all open modal windows
  removeModal();

  // Store the last focused element
  //lastFocusedElement = document.activeElement;

  // Select the modal window
  var modal = document.getElementById('modal');

  // Show the window
  modal.classList.add('modal--visible');

  // Find all focusable children
  var focusableElementsString = 'a[href], area[href], input:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), button:not([disabled]), iframe, object, embed, [tabindex="0"], [tabindex="-1"], [contenteditable]';
  var focusableElements = modal.querySelectorAll(focusableElementsString);

  // Convert NodeList to Array
  focusableElements = Array.prototype.slice.call(focusableElements);

  // The first focusable element within the modal window
  var firstTabStop = focusableElements[0];

  // The last focusable element within the modal window
  var lastTabStop = focusableElements[focusableElements.length - 1];

  // Focus the window
  firstTabStop.focus();

  // Add keydown event
  modal.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {

    // Listen for the Tab key
    if (e.keyCode === 9) {

      // If Shift + Tab
      if (e.shiftKey) {

        // If the current element in focus is the first focusable element within the modal window...
        if (document.activeElement === firstTabStop) {
          e.preventDefault();

          // ...jump to the last focusable element
          lastTabStop.focus();
        }

      // if Tab
      } else {

        // If the current element in focus is the last focusable element within the modal window...
        if (document.activeElement === lastTabStop) {
          e.preventDefault();

          // ...jump to the first focusable element
          firstTabStop.focus();
        }
      }
    }
    
    // Close the window by pressing the Esc-key
    if(e.keyCode === 27) {
      removeModal();
    }
  });
}

// Close the window by clicking the close button
if(closeButton){
   closeButton.addEventListener('click', removeModal);
}

// Remove the modal window if it's visible
function removeModal() {
  var visibleClass = 'modal--visible';
  if (document.querySelector('.' + visibleClass)) {
    document.querySelector('.' + visibleClass).classList.remove(visibleClass);
    // Return the focus to the last focused element
    //lastFocusedElement.focus();
  }
}

function moveModal() {

	var b = document.body;
	var modal = document.getElementById('modal');
	b.appendChild(modal);  

}  
</script>
<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function(event) {     

    if ( window.location.hash.indexOf('de') > -1 ) {
        moveModal();
        showModal();
    }

});
</script>

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {

var swapImg = function() {
    var imgs = jQuery('img.swap');
    var bw = jQuery('body').innerWidth();

    imgs.each(function() {

        var imgNameSmall = jQuery(this).data('src-small');
        var imgNameMedium = jQuery(this).data('src-medium');
        var imgNameLarge = jQuery(this).data('src-large');

        if (bw <= 750) {
            if (imgNameSmall) {
                jQuery(this).attr('src', imgNameSmall);
            } else if (imgNameMedium) {
                jQuery(this).attr('src', imgNameMedium);
            } else {
                jQuery(this).attr('src', imgNameLarge);
            }
        } else if (bw > 750 && bw < 1008) {
            if (imgNameMedium) {
                jQuery(this).attr('src', imgNameMedium);
            } else {
                jQuery(this).attr('src', imgNameLarge);
            }
        } else {
            jQuery(this).attr('src', imgNameLarge);
        }
    });
};


jQuery(document).ready(function() {
    swapImg();
});

jQuery(window).on('resize', swapImg);

 });

</script> </div></div></div></div>

<script>
function runZonegridProducts() {
    var zonegridSlider = jQuery(".zonegrid-products"),
        changeHeight = function() {
            if (jQuery('.zonegrid-products').length) {
                jQuery('.zonegrid-products .product-name').css({'height':'auto'}).equalHeights();
                jQuery('.zonegrid-products .price-wrap').css({'height':'auto'}).equalHeights();
            };
        },
        levelHeights = function() {
            changeHeight();
            jQuery(window).on('delayed-resize', function(e, resizeEvent) {
                changeHeight();
            });
        };
        zonegridSlider.owlCarousel({
          itemsCustom : [
            [0, 2],
            [768, 4],
            [1024, 6]
          ],
          navigation : true,
          pagination: false,
          afterInit : levelHeights
    });
}

jQuery(document).ready(function() {
    runZonegridProducts();
});
</script>

<script> 

         jQuery(document).ready(function() {
           jQuery('.slider-brands').owlCarousel({
             loop: true,
             //margin: 10,
             pagination: false,
             slideSpeed : 800,
             scrollPerPage : true,
            //singleItem: false,
             items: 6,
             slideBy: 6,
             itemsDesktop: [1024, 5],
             itemsDesktopSmall: [900, 4],
             itemsTablet: [768, 3],
             itemsMobile: [600, 2],
           })

           jQuery(".slider-brands").addClass("owl-loaded");

           var owl = jQuery('.slider-brands');
           owl.owlCarousel();

           jQuery(".brands-next").on('click', function() {
             owl.trigger('owl.next');
           });

           jQuery(".brands-prev").on('click', function() {
             owl.trigger('owl.prev');
           });

         })


</script>
<script>

    function equalise() {

      var bw = jQuery('body').innerWidth();	

      function equalheight(container) {

        var currentTallest = 0;
        var currentRowStart = 0;
        var rowDivs = [];
        var $el;
        var topPosition = 0;

        jQuery(container).each(function() {

          $el = jQuery(this);

          jQuery($el).height('auto');
          topPostion = $el.position().top;

          if (currentRowStart != topPostion) {
            for (currentDiv = 0; currentDiv < rowDivs.length; currentDiv++) {
              rowDivs[currentDiv].height(currentTallest);
            }
            rowDivs.length = 0;
            currentRowStart = topPostion;
            currentTallest = $el.height();
            rowDivs.push($el);
          } else {
            rowDivs.push($el);
            currentTallest = (currentTallest < $el.height()) ? ($el.height()) : (currentTallest);
          }

          for (currentDiv = 0; currentDiv < rowDivs.length; currentDiv++) {
            rowDivs[currentDiv].height(currentTallest);
          }
        });
      }

      jQuery(window).load(function() {

      	if (bw > 1024) {
        	equalheight('.equal');
    	}

      });

      jQuery(window).resize(function() {

      	if (bw > 1024) {
        	equalheight('.equal');
    	}

      });
    }


    jQuery(document).ready(function() {
      equalise();
    });


</script>
<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {

var swapImgTimed = function() {

  jQuery(".timed-content").each(function(index) {
    var sRange = jQuery(this).find("time").html();
    var arrTemp = sRange.split(" to ");
    var dtFrom = new Date(arrTemp[0]);
    var dtTo = new Date(arrTemp[1]);
    var dtNow = new Date();

    var bw = window.innerWidth;

    var $this = jQuery(this).find('img');

    var imgNameSmall = jQuery($this).data('src-small');
    var imgNameMedium = jQuery($this).data('src-medium');
    var imgNameLarge = jQuery($this).data('src-large');

    if (dtNow >= dtFrom && dtNow <= dtTo) {

      jQuery(this).closest('.timed-content').show();

      if (bw <= 640) {
        if (imgNameSmall) {
          jQuery($this).attr('src', imgNameSmall);
        } else if (imgNameMedium) {
          jQuery($this).attr('src', imgNameMedium);
        } else {
          jQuery($this).attr('src', imgNameLarge);
        }
      } else if (bw > 640 && bw < 1025) {
        if (imgNameMedium) {
          jQuery($this).attr('src', imgNameMedium);
        } else {
          jQuery($this).attr('src', imgNameLarge);
        }
      } else {
        jQuery($this).attr('src', imgNameLarge);
      }
    }
  });

};

  
swapImgTimed();


jQuery(window).on('resize', swapImgTimed);



});
</script></div> </div>

<h2 id="modal_title">Schließung des deutschen Webshops</h2>
<p>Der deutsche Webshop screwfix.de ist seit dem 29.08.2019 geschlossen. Leider kannst Du keine Online-Bestellungen mehr auf der deutschen Seite tätigen. Stattdessen steht deutschen Kunden ab sofort unser EU Webshop für Bestellungen zur Verfügung. Du erreichst den EU Webshop indem Du dieses Popup-Fenster schließt.</p>
<p>Bitte beachte, dass der EU Webshop ausschließlich in englischer Sprache betrieben wird und ein eigenes EU Produktsortiment führt, das sich vom vorherigen Produktangebot des deutschen Webshops unterscheiden kann. Bereits existierende Daten deutscher Kunden werden nicht auf den EU Webshop übertragen. Um Bestellungen aufzugeben, musst Du ein neues Kundenkonto im EU Webshop eröffnen.</p>
<p><a href="https://www.screwfix.de/site-change">Mehr Informationen</a> zur Schließung des deutschen Webshops und noch offenen Bestellungen.</p>
